Compute misfit (model-observations) cost function. `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:716 ` ` !----------------------------------------------------------------------- `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:717 ` ` ! `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:718 ` `       IF (((time(ng)-0.5_r8*dt(ng)).le.ObsTime(ng)).and.                & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:719 ` `      &    (ObsTime(ng).lt.(time(ng)+0.5_r8*dt(ng)))) THEN `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:720 ` `         ProcessObs=.TRUE. `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:721 ` `         CALL obs_read (ng, iNLM, .FALSE.) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:722 ` ` #   ifdef SP4DVAR `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:723 ` `         IF (iic(ng).ne.ntend(ng)+1.or.Lsadd(ng)) THEN `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:724 ` `           CALL obs_write (ng, tile, iNLM) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:725 ` `           CALL obs_cost (ng, iNLM) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:726 ` `         END IF `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:727 ` ` #   else `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:728 ` `         CALL obs_write (ng, tile, iNLM) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:729 ` ` #    if !(defined R4DVAR \|\| defined VERIFICATION) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:730 ` `         CALL obs_cost (ng, iNLM) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:731 ` ` #    endif `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:732 ` ` #   endif `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:733 ` `       ELSE `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:734 ` `         ProcessObs=.FALSE. `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:735 ` `       END IF `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:736 ` ` #  endif `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:737 ` ` # endif ` | C 문서 누락 | VERIFICATION의 비선형 출력 경로는 OBSERVATIONS가 켜지고 I4DVAR_ANA_SENSITIVITY가 꺼진 조건에서 관측 위치의 모형 값을 기록한다. output.F의 관측 시각 검사와 SP4DVAR 추가 검사를 통과한 경로가 obs_write를 호출한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1621 | [VISC_GRID 설명](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` Options.wiki:3686 ` ` :Option to ` | ` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:218 ` ` ** VISC_GRID               to scale viscosity coefficient by grid size       ** `<br>` ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:70 ` ` #if defined DIFF_GRID \|\| defined DIFF_3DCOEF \|\| \ `<br>` ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:71 ` `     defined VISC_GRID \|\| defined VISC_3DCOEF `<br>` ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:72 ` `      &                   GRID(ng) % grdscl,                             & `<br>` ROMS/Utility/metrics.F:73 ` ` #endif `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:301 ` ` #ifdef VISC_GRID `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:308 ` ` # ifdef UV_VIS2 `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:309 ` `       cff=viscosity2/grdmax(ng) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:312 ` `           visc2_r(i,j)=cff*grdscl(i,j) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:323 ` ` # ifdef UV_VIS4 `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:324 ` `       cff=viscosity4/(grdmax(ng)**3) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/ini_hmixcoef.F:327 ` `           visc4_r(i,j)=cff*grdscl(i,j)**3 ` | C 문서 누락 | VISC_GRID는 UV_VIS2 또는 UV_VIS4의 수평 점성 계수를 격자 크기로 조정한다. ini_hmixcoef는 격자에 따른 값으로 초기 계수를 덮어쓴다. |

ZCLM_NUDGING의 옛 배열은 삭제됐다. git 판정 근거는 ` 63b208cc 2011-04-22 `와 ` 46f20897 2014-03-13 `이다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1738 | [ZCLM_NUDGING 설명](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` Options.wiki:4042 ` ` :Option to nudge SSH climatology data (primarily in sponge areas. ` | ` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:167 ` ` # ifdef ZCLM_NUDGING `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:170 ` `           CLIMA(ng)%Znudgcof(i,j)=Znudg(ng) `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:216 ` ` # ifdef WEST_FSNUDGING `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:217 ` ` #  ifdef ZCLM_NUDGING `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:219 ` `         FSobc_out(ng,iwest)=CLIMA(ng)%Znudgcof(0,1) `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Functionals/ana_nudgcoef.h:220 ` `         FSobc_in (ng,iwest)=obcfac(ng)*FSobc_out(ng,iwest) `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_clima.F:189 ` `       IF (LsshCLM(ng)) THEN `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_clima.F:190 ` `         allocate ( CLIMA(ng) % ssh(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj) ) ` | D 문서에만 있음 | ZCLM_NUDGING을 내부 SSH 기후장 또는 sponge 영역의 nudging 기능으로 설명할 코드 근거는 없다. 옛 Znudgcof는 경계 nudging 계수에 연결됐다. 현재 LsshCLM는 SSH 기후장 자료 처리를 선택한다. |

ifdef BULK_FLUXES `<br>` roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:308 ` ` #  if defined FOUR_DVAR && defined NL_BULK_FLUXES `<br>` roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:309 ` `             IF (Nrun.eq.1) CALL bulk_flux (ng, tile) `<br>` roms-3.6:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:310 ` ` #  if defined FOUR_DVAR && defined PRIOR_BULK_FLUXES `<br>` b1be7141:ROMS/Nonlinear/main3d.F:380 ` `                 IF (Nrun.eq.1) CALL bulk_flux (ng, tile) `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:129 ` ` # ifdef NL_BULK_FLUXES `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:130 ` `       string=uppercase('nl_bulk_fluxes') `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:131 ` `       ic=ic+1 `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:132 ` `       IF (Master) WRITE(stdout,20) TRIM(string),                        & `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:133 ` `      &                             'deprecated option, use ' //         & `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:134 ` `      &                         uppercase('forward_fluxes')//' and ' //  & `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:135 ` `      &                         uppercase('prior_bulk_fluxes') //        & `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:136 ` `      &                             ' instead, FATAL ERROR' `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:3577 ` `       CALL checkadj `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:600 ` `       IF (ic.gt.0) THEN `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:601 ` `         exit_flag=5 `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:602 ` `       END IF `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:606 ` ` #if defined TANGENT \|\| defined TL_IOMS \|\| defined ADJOINT `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:607 ` `  20   FORMAT (/,' CHECKADJ - unsupported option in adjoint-based',      & `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:608 ` `      &        ' algorithms: ',a,/,12x,'REASON: ',a,'.') `<br>` ROMS/Utility/checkadj.F:609 ` ` #endif ` | A 판 차이 | NL_BULK_FLUXES는 옛 비선형 모델의 bulk flux 재사용 옵션이다. 현재 checkadj는 이 이름을 오류로 처리한다. TANGENT·TL_IOMS·ADJOINT 중 하나가 정의된 빌드에서 치명 오류 문구는 FORWARD_FLUXES와 PRIOR_BULK_FLUXES를 제시한다. git 판정 근거는 ` b1be7141 2020-10-12 `이다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1839 | [ANA_MASK cppdefs 목록](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:366 ` ` \|  `<code>&#91;&#91;ANA_MASK&#93;&#93;</code><br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:367 ` ` \| use if analytical Land/Sea masking                     ` | ` roms-2.0:Nonlinear/analytical.F:2625 ` ` #if defined ANA_GRID && defined MASKING `<br>` roms-2.0:Nonlinear/analytical.F:2626 ` `       SUBROUTINE ana_mask (ng, tile) `<br>` roms-3.0:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:143 ` ` #if defined ANA_GRID && defined MASKING `<br>` roms-3.0:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:144 ` ` # include <ana_mask.h> `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:153 ` ` # if defined ANA_GRID && defined MASKING `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:154 ` ` #  include <ana_mask.h> `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:322 ` ` #ifdef ANA_MASK `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:324 ` `       IF (Master) WRITE (stdout,20) 'ANA_MASK',                         & `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/checkdefs.F:325 ` `      &   'Analytical Land/Sea Masking' `<br>` ROMS/Functionals/analytical.F:84 ` ` #ifdef ANALYTICAL ` | B 문서 오기 | 해석적 마스크 계산의 활성 조건은 ANA_GRID와 MASKING이다. analytical.F의 해당 포함문은 ANALYTICAL 안에 있다. ANA_MASK는 옵션 설명과 보고 문자열에 남은 이름이다. 이 이름의 기능 전환을 판 차이로 기록하지 않는다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-1890 | [TKE_WAVEDISS cppdefs 목록](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:548 ` ` \|  `<code>&#91;&#91;TKE_WAVEDISS&#93;&#93;</code><br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:549 ` ` \| use if wave breaking surface flux from wave amplitude  ` | ` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:3 ` ` #if defined NONLINEAR && defined GLS_MIXING && defined SOLVE3D `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:890 ` ` # if defined CRAIG_BANNER `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:896 ` ` # elif defined TKE_WAVEDISS `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:911 ` ` # if defined CHARNOK `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:916 ` ` # elif defined ZOS_HSIG `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:897 ` `           tke(i,j,N(ng),nnew)=MAX(cmu_fac4*(sz_alpha(ng)*               & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:898 ` `      &                            (Dissip_break(i,j)+                   & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:899 ` `      &                             Dissip_wcap (i,j)))**                & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:900 ` `      &                            (2.0_r8/3.0_r8), gls_Kmin(ng)) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:917 ` `           Zos_eff(i)=MAX(zos_hsig_alpha(ng)*Hwave(i,j), Zos_min) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:933 ` ` # if defined CRAIG_BANNER `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:938 ` ` # elif defined TKE_WAVEDISS `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:939 ` `           tke_fluxt(i)=dt(ng)*sz_alpha(ng)*(Dissip_break(i,j)+          & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:940 ` `      &                                      Dissip_wcap(i,j)) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:941 ` ` # else `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:942 ` `           tke_fluxt(i)=0.0_r8 `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/gls_corstep.F:943 ` ` # endif ` | B 문서 오기 | NONLINEAR·GLS_MIXING·SOLVE3D에서 CRAIG_BANNER가 선택되지 않으면, TKE_WAVEDISS는 Dissip_break와 Dissip_wcap의 합으로 표면 TKE 플럭스 항을 설정한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-1897 | [LMD_CONVEC cppdefs 목록](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:582 ` ` \|  `<code>&#91;&#91;LMD_CONVEC&#93;&#93;</code><br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:583 ` ` \| use to add convective mixing due to shear instability  ` | ` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:3 ` ` #if defined NONLINEAR && defined LMD_MIXING && defined SOLVE3D `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:524 ` ` # ifdef LMD_CONVEC `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:521 ` ` !  Compute interior convective diffusivity due to static instability `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:522 ` ` !  mixing. `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:525 ` `             cff=MAX(bvf(i,j,k),lmd_bvfcon) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:526 ` `             cff=MIN(1.0_r8,(lmd_bvfcon-cff)/lmd_bvfcon) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:527 ` `             nu_sxc=1.0_r8-cff*cff `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:528 ` `             nu_sxc=nu_sxc*nu_sxc*nu_sxc `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:536 ` `             Akv(i,j,k)=Akv(i,j,k)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:537 ` `             Akt(i,j,k,itemp)=Akt(i,j,k,itemp)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:538 ` ` # ifdef SALINITY `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:539 ` `             Akt(i,j,k,isalt)=Akt(i,j,k,isalt)+lmd_nu0c*nu_sxc `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/lmd_vmix.F:540 ` ` # endif ` | B 문서 오기 | NONLINEAR·LMD_MIXING·SOLVE3D 경로에서 LMD_CONVEC는 정적 불안정에 의한 대류 혼합을 더한다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-2118 | [헤더 포함·응용 이름](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:3 ` ` Internal header file containing all the  `<code>&#91;&#91;C-preprocessing&#93;&#93;</code>` `<code>&#91;&#91;Options \| options&#93;&#93;</code>`  that defines a particular application. It is included at the top of every ROMS source file. The application CPP option is specified in the  `<code>&#91;&#91;makefile&#93;&#93;</code>`  definition '''ROMS_APPLICATION'''. The application header file is determined during compilation as the lowercase value of '''ROMS_APPLICATION''' with the '''.h''' extension and loaded into the '''ROMS_HEADER''' definition.  Then, during  `<code>&#91;&#91;C-preprocessing&#93;&#93;</code>`  the application header file is included in '''cppdefs.h''' using the following directive: `<br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:5 ` `   <span class="red">#if defined ROMS_HEADER `<br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:6 ` `   # include ROMS_HEADER `<br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:7 ` `   #else `<br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:8 ` `     CPPDEFS - Choose an appropriate ROMS application. `<br>` cppdefs.h.wiki:9 ` `   #endif ` | ` makefile:230 ` ` ifdef ROMS_APPLICATION `<br>` makefile:231 ` `         HEADER := $(addsuffix .h, \ `<br>` makefile:232 ` ` 			$(shell echo ${ROMS_APPLICATION} \| tr [A-Z] [a-z])) `<br>` makefile:234 ` `  ROMS_CPPFLAGS += -D'HEADER="$(HEADER)"' `<br>` makefile:235 ` `  ifdef MY_HEADER_DIR `<br>` makefile:236 ` `   ROMS_CPPFLAGS += -D'ROMS_HEADER="$(MY_HEADER_DIR)/$(HEADER)"' `<br>` makefile:238 ` `   ROMS_CPPFLAGS += -D'ROMS_HEADER="$(HEADER)"' `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:753 ` ` #if defined ROMS_HEADER `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:754 ` ` # include ROMS_HEADER `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:755 ` ` #else `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:756 ` `    CPPDEFS - Choose an appropriate ROMS application. `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:757 ` ` #endif `<br>` ROMS/Utility/yaml_parser.F:1 ` `       MODULE yaml_parser_mod `<br>` ROMS/Programs/types.F:1 ` `       PROGRAM types ` | B 문서 오기 | cppdefs.h를 모델 커널의 공통 전처리 헤더로 설명한다. 모든 .F 파일이 포함한다는 단정을 피한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-2123 | [추적자 이류 기본값](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:79 ` ` :The default horizontal and vertical advection is 4th-order centered. Use the splines vertical advection option is shallow, high vertical resolution applications. ` | ` roms-3.3:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:496 ` ` #  else `<br>` roms-3.3:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:497 ` ` ! Fourth-order, central differences vertical advective flux. `<br>` 1e5c4f15:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1079 ` `           ELSE IF ((Vadvection(itrc,ng)%CENTERED4).or.                  & `<br>` 1e5c4f15:ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:1080 ` `      &             (Vadvection(itrc,ng)%SPLIT_U3)) THEN `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:1818 ` `             Hadvection(itrc,ng)%CENTERED4 = .FALSE. `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:1834 ` `             Vadvection(itrc,ng)%CENTERED4 = .FALSE. `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:2717 ` `           CASE ('C4', 'CENTERED4') `<br>` 57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:2718 ` `             S(itrc,igrid) % CENTERED4 = .TRUE. ` | A 판 차이 | 문서의 수평·수직 4차 중앙차분 기본값은 옛 CPP 선택의 기본 분기에 해당한다. 현재 C4 입력은 해당 CENTERED4 값을 참으로 설정한다. git 판정 근거는 ` 1e5c4f15 2020-01-10 `이다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-2124 | [표면 냉각 중단 조건](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:128 ` ` :Use this option to suppress further surface cooling if the SST is at freezing point or below and the net surface heat flux is cooling: ` | ` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:3 ` ` #ifdef NONLINEAR `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:24 ` ` # ifdef SOLVE3D `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:332 ` ` #  ifdef LIMIT_STFLX_COOLING `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:335 ` ` ! Note: stflx sign convention is that `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:337 ` ` !  positive means heating the ocean (J Wilkin). `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:350 ` `       cff1=-2.0_r8              ! nominal SST threshold to cease cooling `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:353 ` `           cff2=stflx(i,j,itemp) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:354 ` `           cff3=0.5_r8*(1.0_r8+SIGN(1.0_r8,cff1-t(i,j,N(ng),nrhs,itemp))) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/set_vbc.F:355 ` `           stflx(i,j,itemp)=cff2-cff3*0.5_r8*(cff2-ABS(cff2)) ` | C 문서 누락 | NONLINEAR·SOLVE3D의 LIMIT_STFLX_COOLING 분기는 SST가 명목 문턱 -2 °C 이하이고 표면 열 플럭스가 음수일 때 냉각을 없앤다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-2127 | [bulk 플럭스·장파 세 경로](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:179 ` ` :It is possible to compute surface wind stress and surface net heat and freshwater fluxes form atmospheric fields using the bulk flux parameterization of  `<code>&#91;&#91;Bibliography#FairallCW_1996a \| Fairall et al. (1996)&#93;&#93;</code>` . There are three ways to provide longwave radiation in the atmospheric boundary layer: (i) compute the net longwave radiation internally using the Berliand (1952) equation ( `<code>&#91;&#91;LONGWAVE&#93;&#93;</code>` ) as function of air temperature, sea surface temperature, relative humidity, and cloud fraction; (ii) provide (read) longwave downwelling radiation only  and then add outgoing longwave radiation ( `<code>&#91;&#91;LONGWAVE_OUT&#93;&#93;</code>` ) as a function of the model sea surface temperature; and (iii) provide net longwave radiation (default). ` | ` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:14 ` ` !    Fairall, C.W., E.F. Bradley, D.P. Rogers, J.B. Edson and G.S.     ! `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:15 ` ` !      Young, 1996:  Bulk parameterization of air-sea fluxes for       ! `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:16 ` ` !      tropical ocean-global atmosphere Coupled-Ocean Atmosphere       ! `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:17 ` ` !      Response Experiment, JGR, 101, 3747-3764.                       ! `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:285 ` ` # if defined LONGWAVE `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:287 ` ` !  Use Berliand (1952) formula. `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:300 ` ` # elif defined LONGWAVE_OUT `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:305 ` `           LRad(i,j)=lrflx(i,j)*Hscale-                                  & `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:306 ` `      &              emmiss*StefBo*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i) `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:308 ` ` # else `<br>` roms-2.1:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:309 ` `           LRad(i,j)=lrflx(i,j)*Hscale `<br>` f8c2ba60:Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:34 ` ` ! Celsius m/s to W/m2 `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:640 ` `           LRad(i,j)=lrflx(i,j)*Hscale-                                  & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:641 ` `      &              emmiss*StefBo*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i)*TseaK(i) `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:109 ` ` **   There are three ways to provide longwave radiation in the atmospheric   ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:110 ` ` **   boundary layer: (1) Compute the net longwave radiation internally using ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:111 ` ` **   the Berliand (1952) equation (LONGWAVE) as function of air temperature, ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:112 ` ` **   sea surface temperature, relative humidity, and cloud fraction;         ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:113 ` ` **   (2) provide (read) longwave downwelling radiation only  and then add    ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:114 ` ` **   outgoing longwave radiation (LONGWAVE_OUT) as a function of the model   ** `<br>` ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:115 ` ` **   sea surface temperature; (3) provide net longwave radiation (default).  ** ` | A 판 차이 | Fairall(1996)만을 인용한 설명은 ROMS 2.1의 bulk flux 코드와 맞는다. 이후 기본 알고리즘은 COARE 3.0을 거쳐 COARE 3.5로 바뀌었다. 장파 복사의 세 입력 경로는 현재도 유지된다. 현재 COARE_30을 정의하면 COARE 3.0 분기를 선택한다. git 판정 근거는 ` f8c2ba60 2005-05-26 `와 ` a21b63b3 2024-07-10 `이다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-2142 | [DEUGGING 철자](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:1234 ` ` :The CPP option  `<code>&#91;&#91;DEUGGING&#93;&#93;</code>`  is use to avoid writing current date and CPP options to NetCDF file headers. This is used to compare serial and parallel solutions where the UNIX command "diff" is used between NetCDF files. It will only tell us that the binary files are different or not. Finding the parallel bug is completely different story. ` | ` ROMS/Utility/def_info.F:1183 ` ` #ifndef DEBUGGING `<br>` ROMS/Utility/def_info.F:1188 ` `           lstr=LEN_TRIM(Coptions)-1 `<br>` ROMS/Utility/def_info.F:1189 ` `           status=nf90_put_att(ncid, nf90_global, 'CPP_options',         & `<br>` ROMS/Utility/def_info.F:1190 ` `      &                        TRIM(Coptions(1:lstr))) ` | B 문서 오기 | DEUGGING를 DEBUGGING로 정정한다. |

| build-options-history:RBH-3514 | [MY25 문장의 서지 표기](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-build-options-history-vs-code.md) | ` cppdefs.h.wiki:553 ` ` :This is the original closure proposed by  `<code>&#91;&#91;Bibliography#MellorGL_1982 \| Mellor and Yamda (1982)&#93;&#93;</code>`  and latter modified by  `<code>&#91;&#91;Bibliography#GalperinG_1988a \| Galperin et al. (1994)&#93;&#93;</code>` . The default horizontal advection is third-order upstream bias. | inputs:RI1-0632 | [MyAppCPP와 컴파일 매크로](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-inputs-vs-code.md) | ` roms.in.wiki:18 ` ` * C-preprocessing Flag to define the specific configuration.<div class="box">    MyAppCPP = UPWELLING</div>Though this is set by  `<code>&#91;&#91;makefile#ROMS_APPLICATION \| ROMS_APPLICATION&#93;&#93;</code>`  in the  `<code>&#91;&#91;makefile&#93;&#93;</code>`  or  `<code>&#91;&#91;build Script&#93;&#93;</code>` , ROMS is also compiled with <span style="white-space:nowrap;">'''-D$(ROMS_APPLICATION)'''</span>, which allows the use of<div class="box">#ifdef UPWELLING</div>for instance. ROMS is coupled with other external `<br>`       END PROGRAM master     !  software and drivers not written by us `<br>` # endif `<br>` #else `<br>` # include "roms.h" `<br>` #endif `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/master.F:17 ` ` #if defined AIR_OCEAN `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/master.F:18 ` ` # include "air_ocean.h" `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/master.F:19 ` ` #elif defined WAVES_OCEAN `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/master.F:20 ` ` # include "waves_ocean.h" `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/air_ocean.h:53 ` `       CALL mpi_init (MyError) `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/air_ocean.h:68 ` `       CALL read_CouplePar (iNLM) `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/air_ocean.h:81 ` `       CALL ext_mct_ioinit (MyString, MyError) `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/air_ocean.h:82 ` `       CALL mpi_comm_split (MPI_COMM_WORLD, MyColor, MyKey, MyCOMM,      & `<br>` roms-3.0:Master/air_ocean.h:83 ` `      &                     MyError) `<br>` 55c2f307:Master/master.F:20 ` ` #   include "mct_air_ocean.h" `<br>` 9ce141f5:Master/master.F:18 ` ` #  include "mct_coupler.h" `<br>` Master/master.F:16 ` ` #if defined MODEL_COUPLING && !defined CMEPS ` | A 판 차이 | air_ocean.h의 진입 경로는 55c2f307에서 mct_air_ocean.h로 바뀌었다. 9ce141f5는 mct_coupler.h를 도입했다. 4b22f48b는 대기 결합 이름을 ATM_COUPLING으로 바꿨다. 현재 master는 MCT와 ESMF driver를 구분한다. git 판정 근거는 ` 316a75e6 2007-04-27 `와 ` 55c2f307 2008-03-18 `와 ` 9ce141f5 2008-04-01 `와 ` 4b22f48b 2017-07-31 `이다. |
| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-047 | [ANA_PERTURB 기능](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-mixing-bc-forcing-vs-code.md) | ` Options.wiki:260 ` ` :Option to perturb tangent linear and adjoint state initial conditions at specified spatial (i,j,k) location. This is used to determine the correctness of the tangent linear and adjoint models by computing the inner product at the specified location. This inner product must be symmetric within roundoff (A - A<sup>T</sup> = 0). ` | ` ROMS/Functionals/ana_perturb.h:14–25 ` ` !  It is also used to perturb  the tangent linear and adjoint models   ! `<br>` !  at specified state variable and spatial  (i,j,k)  point to verify   ! `<br>` !  the correctness of these algorithms.  This is  activated with the   ! `<br>` !  SANITY_CHECK CPP switch.                                            ! `<br>` !                                                                      ! `<br>` !  If each interior point is  perturbed at one time,  the  resulting   ! `<br>` !  tangent linear (T) and adjoint (A) M-by-N matrices yield:           ! `<br>` !                                                                      ! `<br>` !                T - tranpose(A) = 0    within round off               ! `<br>` !                                                                      ! `<br>` !  That is, their inner product give a symmetric matrix.  Here, M is   ! `<br>` !  the number of state  points and N is the number of perturbations.   ! ` | B 문서 오기 | 코드 주석의 관계는 `T - tranpose(A) = 0    within round off`이다. 문서의 `A - A^T = 0`는 TLM 행렬과 ADM 행렬을 구분하지 않는다. |

| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-848 | [SSW_HUNT_KH·SSW_SOULSBY_KH](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-mixing-bc-forcing-vs-code.md) | ` SoulsbyKH.wiki:5–8 ` ` A fifth-order polynomial approximation attributed to Hunt (1979) by  `<code>&#91;&#91;Bibliography#DeanR_1991a \| Dean and Dalrymple (1991), p72&#93;&#93;</code>`  is used when SSW_HUNT_KH is defined, and the Newton-Raphson method suggested by  `<code>&#91;&#91;Bibliography#SoulsbyR_2005a \| Soulsby (2005)&#93;&#93;</code>`  is used when SSW_SOULSBY_KH is defined.<br><br>{{warning}} '''Note:''' The Soulsby option and these two CPP defs are only  implemented in the version of SSW_BBL.h located in [https://www.myroms.org/projects/cstm/browser/branches/crs/cstm_trunk Sherwood's branch]. Define one of these two options:<br><div class="box">#define SSW_SOULSBY_KH<br />#undef SSW_HUNT_KH</div> ` | ` ROMS/Nonlinear/BBL/ssw_bbl.h:439–447 ` `           Fwave_bot(i,j)=twopi/MAX(Pwave_bot(i,j),1.0_r8) `<br>` #ifdef SSW_CALC_UB `<br>`           Kdh=(h(i,j)+zeta(i,j,nrhs))*Fwave_bot(i,j)**2/g `<br>`           Kbh2=Kdh*Kdh+                                                 & `<br>`      &         Kdh/(1.0_r8+Kdh*(K1+Kdh*(K2+Kdh*(K3+Kdh*(K4+             & `<br>`      &              Kdh*(K5+K6*Kdh)))))) `<br>`           Kbh=SQRT(Kbh2) `<br>`           Ab(i,j)=0.5_r8*Hwave(i,j)/SINH(Kbh)+eps `<br>`           Ub(i,j)=Fwave_bot(i,j)*Ab(i,j)+eps ` | D 문서에만 있음 | 현재 SSW_CALC_UB는 K1부터 K6까지의 계수를 사용하는 다항식을 계산한다. SSW_HUNT_KH와 SSW_SOULSBY_KH로 계산법을 선택하는 기능은 이 저장소에 없다. 위키는 두 옵션의 위치를 Sherwood의 cstm 분기로 한정한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-O020 | [DIAGNOSTICS_BIO](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md) | ` Options.wiki:898 ` ` <section begin=DIAGNOSTICS_BIO />;<span id="DIAGNOSTICS_BIO"></span> `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_BIO&#93;&#93;</code><br>` Options.wiki:899 ` ` :Option to compute and write biological diagnostic terms in the Fennel model. `<br>` Options.wiki:900 ` ` :'''parent = '''  `<code>&#91;&#91;BIO_FENNEL&#93;&#93;</code><br>` Options.wiki:901 ` ` :'''related = '''  `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_TS&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_UV&#93;&#93;</code><br>` Options.wiki:902 ` ` :'''routine = '''  `<code>&#91;&#91;fennel.h&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;wrt_diags.F&#93;&#93;</code>` <section end=DIAGNOSTICS_BIO /> ` | ` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1152 ` ` #if defined DIAGNOSTICS_BIO && \ `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1153 ` `   !(defined BIO_FENNEL      \|\| \ `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1154 ` `     defined ECB             \|\| \ `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1155 ` `     defined ECOSIM          \|\| \ `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1156 ` `     defined HYPOXIA_SRM) `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1157 ` ` #  undef DIAGNOSTICS_BIO `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1158 ` ` #endif ` | C 문서 누락 | BIO_FENNEL 경로는 존재한다. globaldefs.h는 ECB, ECOSIM, HYPOXIA_SRM도 DIAGNOSTICS_BIO 대상으로 인정한다. 위키는 이 추가 대상을 적지 않는다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-O024 | [DIAGNOSTICS_UV](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md) | ` Options.wiki:909 ` ` <section begin=DIAGNOSTICS_UV />;<span id="DIAGNOSTICS_UV"></span> `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_UV&#93;&#93;</code><br>` Options.wiki:910 ` ` :Option to compute and write diagnostic terms for momentum equations balances. `<br>` Options.wiki:911 ` ` :'''related = '''  `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_BIO&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;DIAGNOSTICS_TS&#93;&#93;</code><br>` Options.wiki:912 ` ` :'''routine = '''  `<code>&#91;&#91;set_diags.F&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;step2d.F&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;step3d_t.F&#93;&#93;</code>` ,  `<code>&#91;&#91;wrt_diags.F&#93;&#93;</code>` <section end=DIAGNOSTICS_UV /> ` | ` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1162 ` `     defined DIAGNOSTICS_UV `<br>` ROMS/Include/globaldefs.h:1163 ` ` # define DIAGNOSTICS `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:4 ` ` #if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:364 ` ` # ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_UV `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:365 ` `             DO idiag=1,M3pgrd `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:366 ` `               DiaU3wrk(i,j,k,idiag)=(DiaU3wrk(i,j,k,idiag)+             & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:367 ` `      &                               DC(i,0)*DiaRU(i,j,k,nrhs,idiag))*  & `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:368 ` `      &                              oHz(i,k) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_uv.F:369 ` `             END DO `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/step3d_t.F:12 ` ` !  This routine time-steps tracer equations.  Notice that advective    ! ` | B 문서 오기 | 운동량 진단을 켠다는 설명은 일치한다. 위키 루틴 목록의 step3d_t.F는 트레이서 루틴이다. `#if defined NONLINEAR && defined SOLVE3D` 안에서 `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_UV`가 활성화되면 step3d_uv.F가 3차원 운동량 진단 배열을 갱신한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M032 | [varinfo.dat.wiki:91](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md) | ` varinfo.dat.wiki:91 ` ` Some variables described in varinfo.dat are both input and output variables. The model state variables zeta, temp, salt, u, v…etc. are obviously in this category. Certain surface flux variables can be either input forcing data, or output results computed internally. `<br>` varinfo.dat.wiki:93 ` ` Example: In some ROMS configurations, such as #define BULK_FLUX, surface u-stress, sustr, is an output having been computed internally. It is written to the output NetCDF file if Hout(idUsms) = T in the roms.in file. `<br>` varinfo.dat.wiki:95 ` ` In other configurations sustr is an input, and drives the surface momentum input directly. ` | ` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:3 ` ` #ifdef BULK_FLUXES `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1318 ` `       cff=0.5_r8/rho0 `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1321 ` `           sustr(i,j)=cff*(Taux(i-1,j)+Taux(i,j)) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1336 ` `           svstr(i,j)=cff*(Tauy(i,j-1)+Tauy(i,j)) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1612 ` `             CASE ('Hout(idUsms)') `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1619 ` `               Hout(idUsms,1:Ngrids)=Lswitch(1:Ngrids) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1620 ` `             CASE ('Hout(idVsms)') `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1627 ` `               Hout(idVsms,1:Ngrids)=Lswitch(1:Ngrids) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2202 ` `       IF (Hout(idUsms,ng)) THEN `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2203 ` ` #ifdef SO_SEMI `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2204 ` `         scale=1.0_dp `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2205 ` ` #else `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2206 ` `         scale=rho0                          ! m2/s2 to Pa `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2207 ` ` #endif `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2209 ` `         status=nf_fwrite2d(ng, model, HIS(ng)%ncid, idUsms,             & `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2216 ` `      &                     FORCES(ng) % sustr) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2229 ` `       IF (Hout(idVsms,ng)) THEN ` | B 문서 오기 | 실제 CPP 이름은 BULK_FLUXES다. 위키의 BULK_FLUX는 오기다. bulk_flux.F는 `#ifdef BULK_FLUXES`에서 sustr와 svstr를 계산한다. 이력 출력은 각각 `IF (Hout(idUsms,ng)) THEN`과 `IF (Hout(idVsms,ng)) THEN`으로 선택한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M062 | [varinfo.yaml.wiki:164](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md) | ` varinfo.yaml.wiki:164 ` ` Some field metadata described in '''varinfo.yaml''' are both input and output variables. The model state variables '''zeta''', '''ubar''', '''vbar''', '''u''', '''v''', '''temp''', '''salt''', and '''passive tracers''' are obviously in this category. Also, certain surface flux variables can be either input forcing data, or output results computed internally. `<br>` varinfo.yaml.wiki:166 ` ` For example, in some ROMS configurations when  `<code>&#91;&#91;BULK_FLUX&#93;&#93;</code>`  is activated, surface u-stresses ('''sustr''' and '''svstr''') are output fields computed internally in the surface boundary layer formulation. Such fields are written to the output history NetCDF file if <span class="red">Hout(idUsms) = T</span> and <span class="red">Hout(idVsms) = T</span> in the  `<code>&#91;&#91;roms.in&#93;&#93;</code>`  file.  Otherwise, in other configurations '''sustr''' and '''svstr''' are input that drives the surface momentum forcing directly. ` | ` ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:776 ` `         Fscale(idUsms,ng)=cff*Fscale(idUsms,ng) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:777 ` `         Fscale(idVsms,ng)=cff*Fscale(idVsms,ng) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:3 ` ` #ifdef BULK_FLUXES `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1318 ` `       cff=0.5_r8/rho0 `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1321 ` `           sustr(i,j)=cff*(Taux(i-1,j)+Taux(i,j)) `<br>` ROMS/Nonlinear/bulk_flux.F:1336 ` `           svstr(i,j)=cff*(Tauy(i,j-1)+Tauy(i,j)) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2202 ` `       IF (Hout(idUsms,ng)) THEN `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2203 ` ` #ifdef SO_SEMI `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2204 ` `         scale=1.0_dp `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2205 ` ` #else `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2206 ` `         scale=rho0                          ! m2/s2 to Pa `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2207 ` ` #endif `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2209 ` `         status=nf_fwrite2d(ng, model, HIS(ng)%ncid, idUsms,             & `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2216 ` `      &                     FORCES(ng) % sustr) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:2229 ` `       IF (Hout(idVsms,ng)) THEN ` | B 문서 오기 | 실제 CPP 이름은 BULK_FLUXES다. 위키의 BULK_FLUX는 오기다. bulk_flux.F는 `#ifdef BULK_FLUXES`에서 sustr와 svstr를 계산한다. 이력 출력은 각각 `IF (Hout(idUsms,ng)) THEN`과 `IF (Hout(idVsms,ng)) THEN`으로 선택한다. |

| tools-guides:RTG-024 | [OBC_FILE 설정 안내](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-tools-guides-vs-code.md) | ` Frequently_Asked_Questions.wiki:105 ` ` tell ROMS in  `<code>&#91;&#91;globaldefs.h&#93;&#93;</code>`  that you really, really don't need an  `<code>&#91;&#91;Options#OBC_FILE \| OBC_FILE&#93;&#93;</code> | ` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4837 ` `         ObcData(ng)=.FALSE. `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4839 ` `         ObcData(ng)=ObcData(ng).or.ANY(LBC(:,isFsur,ng)%acquire) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4838 ` ` #ifndef ANA_FSOBC `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4840 ` ` # if defined ADJOINT \|\| defined TANGENT \|\| defined TL_IOMS `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4841 ` `         ObcData(ng)=ObcData(ng).or.ANY(ad_LBC(:,isFsur,ng)%acquire) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4844 ` ` #ifndef ANA_M2OBC `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4846 ` `      &                         .or.ANY(LBC(:,isVbar,ng)%acquire) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4852 ` ` #ifdef SOLVE3D `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4853 ` ` # ifndef ANA_M3OBC `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4861 ` ` # ifndef ANA_TOBC ` | B 문서 오기 | 현재 ROMS는 해석식 경계 옵션과 LBC의 acquire 설정으로 ObcData를 계산한다. OBC_FILE은 현재 경계 파일 선택 옵션이 아니다. |
| tools-guides:RTG-025 | [FSCHAPMAN·M2FLATHER CPP 이름](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-tools-guides-vs-code.md) | ` Frequently_Asked_Questions.wiki:105 ` ` use FSCHAPMAN and M2FLATHER on the open sides of your domain. ` | ` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4839 ` `         ObcData(ng)=ObcData(ng).or.ANY(LBC(:,isFsur,ng)%acquire) `<br>` ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4845 ` `         ObcData(ng)=ObcData(ng).or.ANY(LBC(:,isUbar,ng)%acquire)        & `<br>` roms-3.5:ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:377 ` ` ** EAST_FSCHAPMAN      use if free-surface Chapman condition                 ** `<br>` roms-3.5:ROMS/Include/cppdefs.h:382 ` ` ** EAST_M2FLATHER      use if 2D momentum Flather condition                  ** `<br>` ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1773 ` `             CASE ('CHA') `<br>` ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1774 ` `               S(ib,ifield,igrid)%Chapman_implicit = .TRUE. `<br>` ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1782 ` `             CASE ('FLA') `<br>` ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:1783 ` `               S(ib,ifield,igrid)%Flather = .TRUE. ` | A 판 차이 | FSCHAPMAN과 M2FLATHER 계열 CPP는 ROMS 3.5에 존재한다. 2011-09-23 변경 뒤 LBC의 Cha는 암시적 Chapman을 선택한다. LBC의 Fla는 Flather를 선택한다. git 판정 근거는 ` e8719fb7 2011-09-23 `이다. |
